 6256: 		}

 6872: 	private static bool AreJointConnected(Collider a, Collider b)
 6873: 	{
 6874: 		Rigidbody attachedRigidbody = a.attachedRigidbody;
 6875: 		Rigidbody attachedRigidbody2 = b.attachedRigidbody;
 6876: 		if ((Object)(object)attachedRigidbody == (Object)null || (Object)(object)attachedRigidbody2 == (Object)null)
 6877: 		{
 6878: 			return false;
 6879: 		}
 6880: 		Joint[] components = ((Component)a).GetComponents<Joint>();
 6881: 		foreach (Joint val in components)
 6882: 		{
 6883: 			if ((Object)(object)val.connectedBody == (Object)(object)attachedRigidbody2)
 6884: 			{
 6885: 				return true;
 6886: 			}
 6887: 		}
 6888: 		Joint[] components2 = ((Component)b).GetComponents<Joint>();
 6889: 		foreach (Joint val2 in components2)
 6890: 		{
 6891: 			if ((Object)(object)val2.connectedBody == (Object)(object)attachedRigidbody)
 6892: 			{
 6893: 				return true;
 6894: 			}
 6895: 		}
 6896: 		return false;
 6897: 	}

  282: 			if (base.currentSearch.inProgress)
  283: 			{
  284: 				((EnemyAI)this).StopSearch(base.currentSearch, true);
  285: 			}
  286: 			base.timeSinceSpawn += Time.deltaTime;
  287: 			return;
  288: 		}
  289: 		if (base.isEnemyDead)
  290: 		{
  291: 			((Behaviour)base.agent).enabled = false;
  292: 			return;
  293: 		}
  294: 		if (base.movingTowardsTargetPlayer && (Object)(object)base.targetPlayer != (Object)null)
  295: 		{
  296: 			((EnemyAI)this).NavigateTowardsTargetPlayer();
  297: 		}
  298: 		if (!base.inSpecialAnimation)
  299: 		{
  300: 			if (base.updateDestinationInterval >= 0f)
  301: 			{
  302: 				base.updateDestinationInterval -= Time.deltaTime;
  303: 				return;
  304: 			}
  305: 			((EnemyAI)this).DoAIInterval();
  306: 			base.updateDestinationInterval = base.AIIntervalTime + Random.Range(-0.015f, 0.015f);
  307: 		}
  308: 	}

  310: 	public override void DoAIInterval()
  311: 	{
  312: 		((EnemyAI)this).DoAIInterval();
  313: 		if ((Object)(object)Leader != (Object)null && Leader.AI.isEnemyDead)
  314: 		{
  315: 			LethalMin.Logger.LogInfo((object)(DebugID + ": Leader is dead! Turning back to normal..."));
  316: 			TransformIntoPikminServerRpc();
  317: 			return;
  318: 		}
  319: 		if ((Object)(object)Leader == (Object)null && (Object)(object)PreviousLeader != (Object)null && PreviousLeader.AI.isEnemyDead)
  320: 		{
  321: 			LethalMin.Logger.LogInfo((object)(DebugID + ": Previous Leader is dead! Turning back to normal..."));
  322: 			TransformIntoPikminServerRpc();
  323: 			return;
  324: 		}
  325: 		switch (base.currentBehaviourStateIndex)
  326: 		{
  327: 		case 0:
  328: 			HandleIdleStateOnOwnerClient();
  329: 			break;
  330: 		case 1:
  331: 			HandleFollowStateOnOwnerClient();
  332: 			break;
  333: 		case 2:
  334: 			HandleChaseStateOnOwnerClient();
  335: 			break;
  336: 		}
  337: 	}

  674: 	public IEnumerator TryHitPlayer(PlayerControllerB player)
  675: 	{

  677: 		base.creatureAnimator.SetTrigger("Attack");

  679: 		yield return (object)new WaitForSeconds(0.25f);
  680: 		if ((double)Vector3.Distance(((Component)this).transform.position, ((Component)player).transform.position) < 2.5)
  681: 		{
  682: 			if (((NetworkBehaviour)player).IsOwner)
  683: 			{
  684: 				player.DamagePlayer(1, true, true, (CauseOfDeath)1, 0, false, ((Component)this).transform.forward);
  685: 			}
  686: 			base.creatureSFX.PlayOneShot(HitSound);
  687: 		}

  690: 	public void SetPuffminAsLatchedOn(Leader leader)
  691: 	{
  692: 		LethalMin.Logger.LogInfo((object)(DebugID + ": Setting as latched on to " + leader.Controller.playerUsername));
  693: 		if (latchedRoutine != null)
  694: 		{
  695: 			((MonoBehaviour)this).StopCoroutine(latchedRoutine);
  696: 			latchedRoutine = null;
  697: 		}
  698: 		LeaderLatchedTo = leader;
  699: 		SetCollisionMode(0);
  700: 		((EnemyAI)this).SwitchToBehaviourStateOnLocalClient(2);
  701: 		SwitchToIntent(PuffminIntent.LatchedOn);
  702: 		latchedRoutine = ((MonoBehaviour)this).StartCoroutine(LatchedOnRoutine());
  703: 	}
  704: 
  705: 	public void UnLatchPuffmin()
  706: 	{
  707: 		if (latchedRoutine != null)
  708: 		{
  709: 			LethalMin.Logger.LogInfo((object)(DebugID + " stopping latch routine"));
  710: 			((MonoBehaviour)this).StopCoroutine(latchedRoutine);
  711: 			latchedRoutine = null;
  712: 		}
  713: 		if ((Object)(object)LeaderLatchedTo != (Object)null)
  714: 		{
  715: 			LethalMin.Logger.LogInfo((object)$"{DebugID}: Unlatching from {((NetworkBehaviour)LeaderLatchedTo).OwnerClientId}");
  716: 			if (LeaderLatchedTo.PuffminLatchedOn.ContainsKey(this))
  717: 			{
  718: 				Object.Destroy((Object)(object)((Component)LeaderLatchedTo.PuffminLatchedOn[this]).gameObject);
  719: 				LeaderLatchedTo.PuffminLatchedOn.Remove(this);
  720: 			}
  721: 			else
  722: 			{
  723: 				LethalMin.Logger.LogError((object)(DebugID + ": Failed to find self in LeaderLatchedTo's PuffminLatchedOn!"));
  724: 			}
  725: 		}
  726: 		LeaderLatchedTo = null;

   18: 	private static IEnumerable<CodeInstruction> UpdateTranspiler(IEnumerable<CodeInstruction> instructions)
   19: 	{
   20: 		MethodInfo despawnMethod = AccessTools.Method(typeof(NetworkObject), "Despawn", new Type[1] { typeof(bool) }, (Type[])null);
   21: 		MethodInfo handleMethod = AccessTools.Method(typeof(DontTouchMeUpdateTranspiler), "HandleEnemyClear", (Type[])null, (Type[])null);
   22: 		bool patched = false;

   24: 		{
   25: 			if (!patched && CodeInstructionExtensions.Calls(code, despawnMethod))

   29: 			}

   33: 			}
   34: 		}
   35: 		if (!patched)
   36: 		{
   37: 			LethalMin.Logger.LogWarning((object)"[DTMCompat] UpdateTranspiler: NetworkObject.Despawn not found in DTMEntityAI.Update — Pikmin may be hard-despawned by the monster-clear loop. Consider disabling 'Pikmin Friendly Despawn' until a fix is available.");
   38: 		}
   39: 	}
